==> README.md <==
# The Boe Cottage — Reservasi & Channel Manager

Platform reservasi cottage di Dieng berbasis **Laravel 12 + Inertia.js + React 19 + Tailwind CSS v4 (Vite)**, lengkap dengan:

- Mesin booking atomik (anti double-booking) + harga dihitung di server (termasuk seasonal rate & service fee Rp370.000)
- Pembayaran **Midtrans Snap** (mode fallback `MOCK-SNAP-*` otomatis jika key kosong)
- Sinkronisasi iCal 2 arah untuk **Airbnb & Tiket.com** (import + export `.ics`)
- Admin Dashboard & Central Bookings dengan autentikasi session

---

## 1. Cara Setup Cepat

**Prasyarat:** PHP 8.2+ (ekstensi `pdo_sqlite`), Composer 2, Node.js 20+ & npm. (Pengguna Laragon: pastikan folder `bin\php` dan `bin\nodejs` Laragon sudah masuk `PATH`.)

```bash
# 1. Install dependency backend & frontend
composer install
npm install

# 2. Siapkan file environment
cp .env.example .env            # Windows CMD: copy .env.example .env
php artisan key:generate

# 3. Buat file database SQLite (jika belum ada), lalu migrasi + seed data contoh
php -r "file_exists('database/database.sqlite') || touch('database/database.sqlite');"
php artisan migrate:fresh --seed

# 4. Jalankan Laravel server + Vite dev server sekaligus
composer run dev
```

Buka **http://localhost:8000**.

> Tidak perlu MySQL maupun key Midtrans untuk development. Dengan `MIDTRANS_SERVER_KEY` kosong, booking tetap berhasil dan mengembalikan token `MOCK-SNAP-<kode booking>`.

Perintah lain yang berguna:

| Perintah | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `php artisan test` | Menjalankan seluruh test suite (24 test E2E) |
| `npm run build` | Build aset produksi ke `public/build` |
| `php artisan sync:ical` | Sinkronisasi manual feed iCal OTA |

---

## 3. Panduan Penting Tim Frontend

### Lokasi File

```
resources/js/
├── app.jsx                 # Entry point Inertia (resolve halaman dari Pages/)
├── bootstrap.js            # Konfigurasi global window.axios (CSRF, header) — JANGAN dihapus
├── Pages/                  # Satu file = satu halaman (dirender dari routes/web.php)
│   ├── Welcome.jsx         # Landing page  →  GET /
│   ├── Reservation.jsx     # Booking + pembayaran Midtrans  →  GET /reservation
│   └── Admin/
│       ├── AdminDashboard.jsx   # GET /admin-dashboard (butuh login)
│       └── CentralBookings.jsx  # GET /central-bookings (butuh login)
└── Components/             # Komponen yang dipakai ulang
    ├── AboutSection.jsx, LocationSection.jsx, RevealOnScroll.jsx
    ├── RoomSection.jsx, RoomDetailModal.jsx, ReviewSection.jsx
    ├── Footer.jsx          # Berisi form kirim review (POST /api/reviews)
    └── LoginModal.jsx      # Form login admin (POST /login)
```

Gambar statis ada di `public/images/cottage/`. CSS global/Tailwind ada di `resources/css/app.css`.

### Inertia Props dari Backend

Props didefinisikan di [`routes/web.php`](routes/web.php). Gunakan nama props persis seperti di bawah.

**Shared props (tersedia di semua halaman via `usePage().props`)** — dari `app/Http/Middleware/HandleInertiaRequests.php`:

| Prop | Isi |
|---|---|
| `auth.user` | `{ id, name, email }` atau `null` jika belum login |
| `midtrans` | `{ client_key, is_production }` untuk memuat `snap.js` |

**`Pages/Welcome.jsx`**

| Prop | Isi |
|---|---|
| `rooms` | Array room + relasi `images` (`id, name, slug, description, price_per_night, capacity, status, size, amenities, images[]`) |
| `reviews` | Array review yang sudah disetujui (`guest_name, rating, comment, created_at`) |

**`Pages/Reservation.jsx`**

| Prop | Isi |
|---|---|
| `rooms` | Array room + `images` (sama seperti Welcome) |
| `seasonalRates` | Aturan harga musiman aktif (`rule_name, room_id, start_date, end_date, rate_adjustment_percent`) — hanya untuk tampilan estimasi, **harga final tetap dihitung server** |

**`Pages/Admin/AdminDashboard.jsx`**

| Prop | Isi |
|---|---|
| `rooms` | Room + `images` + `bookings` aktif ke depan, plus `export_url` & `export_token` (URL feed `.ics` untuk Airbnb/Tiket.com) |
| `arrivalsToday` | Booking yang check-in hari ini (+ relasi `room`) |
| `seasonalRates` | Seasonal rate aktif (+ relasi `room`) |
| `icalLinks` | Link import iCal OTA (`platform, url, is_active, last_synced_at, room`) |
| `dashboardStats` | `{ revenue: { amount, raw_amount, target, bookings }, occupancy: { amount, units, percent }, arrivals: { parties, guests, pending } }` |

**`Pages/Admin/CentralBookings.jsx`**

| Prop | Isi |
|---|---|
| `bookings` | Semua booking (+ relasi `room`): `booking_code, guest_*, check_in, check_out, adults, children, total_price, source, status, payment_status, check_in_status, ...` |
| `rooms` | Array room + `images` |

### Endpoint API yang Sudah Terhubung

| Dipakai di | Method & URL | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| Reservation.jsx | `GET /api/rooms/{id}/booked-dates` | Tanggal yang tidak bisa dipilih di kalender |
| Reservation.jsx | `POST /api/bookings` | Membuat booking + mengembalikan `snap_token` |
| Footer.jsx | `POST /api/reviews` | Kirim review tamu |
| LoginModal.jsx | `POST /login` | Login admin |
| Admin pages | `POST /logout` | Logout |
| AdminDashboard.jsx | `PUT /admin/rooms/{id}`, `PATCH /admin/rooms/{id}/status` | Edit room & status (AVAILABLE / OCCUPIED / MAINTENANCE) |
| AdminDashboard.jsx | `POST /admin/seasonal-rates` | Tambah seasonal rate |
| AdminDashboard.jsx | `PATCH /admin/bookings/{id}` | Update status check-in |
| AdminDashboard.jsx | `POST /admin/ical-links`, `DELETE /admin/ical-links/{id}`, `POST /admin/ical-links/sync` | Kelola & sinkronisasi channel OTA |
| CentralBookings.jsx | `POST /admin/bookings` | Reservasi manual (WhatsApp / walk-in) |

> Endpoint publik dibatasi rate limit (`throttle:60,1` untuk baca, `throttle:15,1` untuk booking/review/login). Jika muncul error **429**, tunggu 1 menit.

### ⚠️ Peringatan — Jangan Dihapus / Diubah Tanpa Koordinasi Backend

1. **State & fungsi Midtrans di `Reservation.jsx`** — loader `snap.js` (berdasarkan `midtrans.client_key` & `midtrans.is_production`), pemanggilan `POST /api/bookings`, dan `window.snap.pay(snapToken, { onSuccess, onPending, onError, onClose })`. Menghapusnya akan memutus alur pembayaran.
2. **Semua pemanggilan `window.axios`** di Pages & Components. Silakan ubah tampilan (JSX/Tailwind), tetapi pertahankan URL endpoint, method, dan nama field payload (`room_id, check_in, check_out, adults, children, guest_name, guest_email, guest_phone, notes`, dst).
3. **`resources/js/bootstrap.js`** — mengatur header CSRF/`X-Requested-With` untuk axios. Tanpa ini request POST/PATCH akan gagal (419).
4. **Jangan menghitung harga final di frontend.** Total harga, seasonal rate, dan service fee dihitung di `BookingController`; frontend hanya menampilkan estimasi dan respons `pricing` dari server.
5. **Nama props Inertia** di tabel atas adalah kontrak dengan backend. Jika butuh data tambahan, minta tim backend menambahkannya di `routes/web.php`.

---

## 4. Struktur Backend Singkat (Referensi)

| Lokasi | Isi |
|---|---|
| `routes/web.php`, `routes/api.php` | Definisi halaman, props Inertia & endpoint |
| `app/Http/Controllers/` | `BookingController`, `AuthController`, `RoomController`, `AvailabilityController`, `IcalController` |
| `app/Services/` | `MidtransService` (Snap API + verifikasi webhook), `IcalSyncService` (import OTA) |
| `routes/console.php` | Scheduler: `sync:ical` (15 menit) & `bookings:expire-unpaid` (5 menit) |
| `database/seeders/DatabaseSeeder.php` | Admin, Daisy Room, Lily Room, review & booking contoh |
| `tests/Feature/BoeCottageE2ETest.php` | Test end-to-end seluruh alur |
| `.env.production.example`, `deploy.sh` | Template & skrip deployment produksi |

==> app/Console/Commands/SyncIcalCommand.php <==
<?php

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Models\IcalLink;
use App\Services\IcalSyncService;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;

class SyncIcalCommand extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'sync:ical';
    protected $description = 'Sync kalender booking dari Airbnb, Tiket.com & OTA lainnya via iCal';

    public function handle(IcalSyncService $syncService): int
    {
        $links = IcalLink::where('is_active', true)->get();
        $this->info("Memulai sinkronisasi " . $links->count() . " link iCal aktif...");

        $totals = [
            'imported' => 0,
            'updated' => 0,
            'cancelled' => 0,
            'errors' => 0,
        ];

        foreach ($links as $link) {
            $this->info("Syncing room ID: {$link->room_id} ({$link->platform})...");
            $result = $syncService->sync($link);

            $totals['imported'] += $result['imported'];
            $totals['updated'] += $result['updated'];
            $totals['cancelled'] += $result['cancelled'];
            $totals['errors'] += $result['errors'];

            if ($result['errors'] === 0) {
                $this->info("Berhasil sync! (+{$result['imported']} baru, {$result['updated']} diperbarui, {$result['cancelled']} dibatalkan)");
            } else {
                $this->error("Terdapat {$result['errors']} kendala saat mengambil/memproses URL.");
            }
        }

        $this->info("Proses sync selesai! Total: {$totals['imported']} imported, {$totals['updated']} updated, {$totals['cancelled']} cancelled, {$totals['errors']} errors.");

        return self::SUCCESS;
    }
}

==> app/Http/Controllers/AuthController.php <==
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class AuthController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Handle session login request.
     * POST /login
     */
    public function login(Request $request)
    {
        $credentials = $request->validate([
            'email' => ['required', 'email'],
            'password' => ['required', 'string'],
        ]);

        if (!Auth::attempt($credentials, $request->boolean('remember'))) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'email' => ['Invalid email or password.'],
            ]);
        }

        $request->session()->regenerate();

        if ($request->expectsJson()) {
            return response()->json([
                'user' => Auth::user(),
                'redirect' => '/admin-dashboard',
            ]);
        }

        return redirect()->intended('/admin-dashboard');
    }

    /**
     * Handle session logout request.
     * POST /logout
     */
    public function logout(Request $request)
    {
        Auth::guard('web')->logout();

        $request->session()->invalidate();
        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        if ($request->expectsJson()) {
            return response()->json([
                'redirect' => '/',
            ]);
        }

        return redirect('/');
    }
}


==> app/Http/Controllers/AvailabilityController.php <==
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Booking;
use App\Models\Room;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class AvailabilityController extends Controller
{
    // Mengambil daftar tanggal yang sudah ter-booking untuk 1 kamar
    public function getBookedDates(Room $room)
    {
        $bookedDates = Booking::where('room_id', $room->id)
            ->whereIn('status', ['confirmed', 'pending'])
            ->get(['check_in', 'check_out'])
            ->flatMap(function ($booking) {
                $checkIn = $booking->check_in instanceof \DateTimeInterface
                    ? $booking->check_in->format('Y-m-d')
                    : (string) $booking->check_in;
                $checkOut = $booking->check_out instanceof \DateTimeInterface
                    ? $booking->check_out->format('Y-m-d')
                    : (string) $booking->check_out;

                $period = new \DatePeriod(
                    new \DateTime($checkIn),
                    new \DateInterval('P1D'),
                    new \DateTime($checkOut)
                );

                $dates = [];
                foreach ($period as $date) {
                    $dates[] = $date->format('Y-m-d');
                }
                return $dates;
            })
            ->unique()
            ->values();

        return response()->json([
            'room_id' => $room->id,
            'room_status' => $room->status,
            'disabled_dates' => $bookedDates,
        ]);
    }

    // Validasi apakah range tanggal yang dipilih tamu valid (tidak bentrok)
    public function checkOverlap(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'room_id' => 'required|exists:rooms,id',
            'check_in' => 'required|date|after_or_equal:today',
            'check_out' => 'required|date|after:check_in',
        ]);

        $room = Room::findOrFail($request->room_id);
        if ($room->status === 'MAINTENANCE') {
            return response()->json([
                'available' => false,
                'message' => 'Kamar sedang dalam pemeliharaan (maintenance).',
            ]);
        }

        $isOverlap = Booking::where('room_id', $request->room_id)
            ->whereIn('status', ['confirmed', 'pending'])
            ->where(function ($query) use ($request) {
                $query->where('check_in', '<', $request->check_out)
                      ->where('check_out', '>', $request->check_in);
            })
            ->exists();

        return response()->json([
            'available' => !$isOverlap,
            'message' => $isOverlap ? 'Tanggal sudah dipesan, silakan pilih tanggal lain.' : 'Kamar tersedia!',
        ]);
    }
}

==> app/Http/Controllers/BookingController.php <==
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Booking;
use App\Models\Room;
use App\Models\SeasonalRate;
use App\Services\MidtransService;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class BookingController extends Controller
{
    public const SERVICE_FEE = 370000;

    /**
     * Atomic guest booking creation + Midtrans Snap token generation.
     * POST /api/bookings
     */
    public function store(Request $request, MidtransService $midtransService)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'room_id' => 'required|exists:rooms,id',
            'check_in' => 'required|date|after_or_equal:today',
            'check_out' => 'required|date|after:check_in',
            'adults' => 'nullable|integer|min:1|max:12',
            'children' => 'nullable|integer|min:0|max:10',
            'guest_name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'guest_email' => 'required|email|max:255',
            'guest_phone' => 'nullable|string|max:50',
            'notes' => 'nullable|string|max:2000',
        ]);

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($validated, $midtransService) {
            /** @var Room $room */
            $room = Room::where('id', $validated['room_id'])->lockForUpdate()->firstOrFail();

            if ($room->status === 'MAINTENANCE') {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                    'room_id' => ['This cottage is currently undergoing maintenance and cannot be booked.'],
                ]);
            }

            $isOverlap = Booking::where('room_id', $room->id)
                ->whereIn('status', ['confirmed', 'pending'])
                ->where(function ($query) use ($validated) {
                    $query->where('check_in', '<', $validated['check_out'])
                        ->where('check_out', '>', $validated['check_in']);
                })
                ->exists();

            if ($isOverlap) {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                    'check_in' => ['Selected dates conflict with an existing reservation. Please choose different dates.'],
                ]);
            }

            $pricing = $this->calculateStayPrice($room, $validated['check_in'], $validated['check_out'], true);

            do {
                $bookingCode = 'BOE-' . now()->format('Ymd') . '-' . Str::upper(Str::random(4));
            } while (Booking::where('booking_code', $bookingCode)->exists());

            $booking = Booking::create([
                'booking_code' => $bookingCode,
                'room_id' => $room->id,
                'guest_name' => $validated['guest_name'],
                'guest_email' => $validated['guest_email'],
                'guest_phone' => $validated['guest_phone'] ?? null,
                'check_in' => $validated['check_in'],
                'check_out' => $validated['check_out'],
                'adults' => $validated['adults'] ?? 1,
                'children' => $validated['children'] ?? 0,
                'notes' => $validated['notes'] ?? null,
                'total_price' => $pricing['total'],
                'source' => 'direct',
                'status' => 'pending',
                'check_in_status' => 'pending_arrival',
                'payment_status' => 'unpaid',
            ]);

            $snapToken = $midtransService->createSnapToken($booking, $room, $pricing['items']);
            $booking->update(['snap_token' => $snapToken]);

            return response()->json([
                'booking' => $booking->fresh('room'),
                'snap_token' => $snapToken,
                'pricing' => $pricing,
            ], 201);
        });
    }

    /**
     * Admin manual reservation creation (Central Bookings).
     * POST /api/admin/bookings
     */
    public function storeManual(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'room_id' => 'required|exists:rooms,id',
            'guest_name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'guest_email' => 'nullable|email|max:255',
            'guest_phone' => 'required|string|max:50',
            'check_in' => 'required|date',
            'check_out' => 'required|date|after:check_in',
            'adults' => 'nullable|integer|min:1|max:12',
            'children' => 'nullable|integer|min:0|max:10',
            'source' => 'nullable|in:direct,airbnb,tiket_com,booking_com,agoda,whatsapp,manual',
            'status' => 'nullable|in:pending,confirmed,cancelled',
            'payment_status' => 'nullable|in:unpaid,pending,paid,expired,failed',
            'payment_type' => 'nullable|string|max:50',
            'notes' => 'nullable|string|max:2000',
        ]);

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($validated) {
            /** @var Room $room */
            $room = Room::where('id', $validated['room_id'])->lockForUpdate()->firstOrFail();

            $isOverlap = Booking::where('room_id', $room->id)
                ->whereIn('status', ['confirmed', 'pending'])
                ->where(function ($query) use ($validated) {
                    $query->where('check_in', '<', $validated['check_out'])
                        ->where('check_out', '>', $validated['check_in']);
                })
                ->exists();

            if ($isOverlap) {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                    'check_in' => ['Selected dates conflict with an existing reservation for this room.'],
                ]);
            }

            $pricing = $this->calculateStayPrice($room, $validated['check_in'], $validated['check_out'], true);

            do {
                $bookingCode = 'BOE-' . now()->format('Ymd') . '-' . Str::upper(Str::random(4));
            } while (Booking::where('booking_code', $bookingCode)->exists());

            $paymentStatus = $validated['payment_status'] ?? 'paid';
            $status = $validated['status'] ?? ($paymentStatus === 'paid' ? 'confirmed' : 'pending');

            $booking = Booking::create([
                'booking_code' => $bookingCode,
                'room_id' => $room->id,
                'guest_name' => $validated['guest_name'],
                'guest_email' => $validated['guest_email'] ?? null,
                'guest_phone' => $validated['guest_phone'],
                'check_in' => $validated['check_in'],
                'check_out' => $validated['check_out'],
                'adults' => $validated['adults'] ?? 2,
                'children' => $validated['children'] ?? 0,
                'notes' => $validated['notes'] ?? null,
                'total_price' => $pricing['total'],
                'source' => $validated['source'] ?? 'manual',
                'status' => $status,
                'check_in_status' => 'pending_arrival',
                'payment_status' => $paymentStatus,
                'payment_type' => $validated['payment_type'] ?? 'Manual / Bank Transfer',
                'paid_at' => $paymentStatus === 'paid' ? now() : null,
            ]);

            return response()->json([
                'booking' => $booking->fresh('room'),
                'message' => 'Reservation created successfully.',
            ], 201);
        });
    }

    /**
     * Update booking check-in or reservation status from Admin Dashboard / Central Bookings.
     * PATCH /api/admin/bookings/{booking}
     */
    public function updateStatus(Request $request, Booking $booking)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'status' => 'nullable|in:pending,confirmed,cancelled',
            'check_in_status' => 'nullable|in:pending_arrival,checked_in,checked_out',
            'payment_status' => 'nullable|in:unpaid,pending,paid,expired,failed',
        ]);

        if (isset($validated['payment_status']) && $validated['payment_status'] === 'paid' && !$booking->paid_at) {
            $validated['paid_at'] = now();
        }

        $booking->update(array_filter($validated, fn ($val) => !is_null($val)));

        return response()->json([
            'booking' => $booking->fresh('room'),
            'message' => 'Booking updated successfully.',
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Midtrans payment notification webhook.
     * POST /api/midtrans/webhook
     */
    public function webhook(Request $request, MidtransService $midtransService)
    {
        $orderId = (string) $request->input('order_id', '');
        $statusCode = (string) $request->input('status_code', '');
        $grossAmount = (string) $request->input('gross_amount', '');
        $signatureKey = (string) $request->input('signature_key', '');
        $transactionStatus = (string) $request->input('transaction_status', '');
        $fraudStatus = (string) $request->input('fraud_status', 'accept');

        if (!$midtransService->verifySignature($orderId, $statusCode, $grossAmount, $signatureKey)) {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'Invalid Midtrans signature'], 403);
        }

        $booking = Booking::where('booking_code', $orderId)->first();
        if (!$booking) {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'Booking not found'], 404);
        }

        $updates = [
            'payment_type' => $request->input('payment_type', $booking->payment_type),
            'midtrans_transaction_id' => $request->input('transaction_id', $booking->midtrans_transaction_id),
        ];

        if ($transactionStatus === 'capture') {
            if ($fraudStatus === 'accept') {
                $updates['payment_status'] = 'paid';
                $updates['status'] = 'confirmed';
                $updates['paid_at'] = now();
            }
        } elseif ($transactionStatus === 'settlement') {
            $updates['payment_status'] = 'paid';
            $updates['status'] = 'confirmed';
            $updates['paid_at'] = now();
        } elseif ($transactionStatus === 'pending') {
            $updates['payment_status'] = 'pending';
            $updates['status'] = 'pending';
        } elseif (in_array($transactionStatus, ['cancel', 'deny', 'expire'], true)) {
            $updates['payment_status'] = 'expired';
            $updates['status'] = 'cancelled';
        }

        $booking->update($updates);

        return response()->json([
            'message' => 'Webhook processed',
            'booking_code' => $booking->booking_code,
            'status' => $booking->status,
            'payment_status' => $booking->payment_status,
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Calculate stay price including active seasonal rate rules and service fee.
     */
    public function calculateStayPrice(Room $room, string $checkIn, string $checkOut, bool $includeServiceFee = true): array
    {
        $period = new \DatePeriod(
            new \DateTime($checkIn),
            new \DateInterval('P1D'),
            new \DateTime($checkOut)
        );

        $seasonalRules = SeasonalRate::where('is_active', true)
            ->where(function ($query) use ($room) {
                $query->whereNull('room_id')
                    ->orWhere('room_id', $room->id);
            })
            ->where('start_date', '<=', $checkOut)
            ->where('end_date', '>=', $checkIn)
            ->get();

        $basePrice = (float) $room->price_per_night;
        $nights = 0;
        $baseSubtotal = 0.0;
        $seasonalAdjustment = 0.0;

        foreach ($period as $date) {
            $nights++;
            $dateStr = $date->format('Y-m-d');
            $baseSubtotal += $basePrice;

            foreach ($seasonalRules as $rule) {
                $ruleStart = $rule->start_date->format('Y-m-d');
                $ruleEnd = $rule->end_date->format('Y-m-d');
                if ($dateStr >= $ruleStart && $dateStr <= $ruleEnd) {
                    $seasonalAdjustment += $basePrice * ((float) $rule->rate_adjustment_percent / 100.0);
                }
            }
        }

        $serviceFee = ($nights > 0 && $includeServiceFee) ? self::SERVICE_FEE : 0;
        $total = round($baseSubtotal + $seasonalAdjustment + $serviceFee);

        $items = [
            [
                'id' => 'ROOM-' . $room->id,
                'price' => (int) round($basePrice),
                'quantity' => $nights,
                'name' => "{$room->name} ({$nights} Nights)",
            ],
        ];

        if ((int) round($seasonalAdjustment) !== 0) {
            $items[] = [
                'id' => 'SEASONAL-ADJ',
                'price' => (int) round($seasonalAdjustment),
                'quantity' => 1,
                'name' => 'Seasonal Rate Adjustment',
            ];
        }

        if ($serviceFee > 0) {
            $items[] = [
                'id' => 'SERVICE-FEE',
                'price' => (int) $serviceFee,
                'quantity' => 1,
                'name' => 'Taxes & Hearth Service',
            ];
        }

        return [
            'nights' => $nights,
            'base_subtotal' => round($baseSubtotal),
            'seasonal_adjustment' => round($seasonalAdjustment),
            'service_fee' => $serviceFee,
            'total' => $total,
            'items' => $items,
        ];
    }
}

==> app/Http/Controllers/IcalController.php <==
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Booking;
use App\Models\IcalLink;
use App\Models\Room;
use App\Services\IcalSyncService;
use DateTimeImmutable;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Sabre\VObject\Component\VCalendar;

class IcalController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Public Outbound iCal (.ics) Export Feed for Airbnb, Tiket.com, etc.
     * GET /ical/export/{room:slug}.ics
     */
    public function export(Request $request, Room $room)
    {
        $providedToken = (string) $request->query('token', '');
        $expectedToken = $room->export_token;
        $customTokens = $room->icalLinks()->whereNotNull('export_token')->pluck('export_token')->all();

        $validTokens = array_filter(array_unique([$expectedToken, ...$customTokens]));

        if ($providedToken !== '' && !in_array($providedToken, $validTokens, true)) {
            abort(403, 'Invalid iCal export token.');
        }

        $vcalendar = new VCalendar([
            'PRODID' => '-//The Boe Cottage//Channel Manager//EN',
            'VERSION' => '2.0',
            'CALSCALE' => 'GREGORIAN',
        ]);

        $bookings = Booking::where('room_id', $room->id)
            ->whereIn('status', ['confirmed', 'pending'])
            ->where('check_out', '>=', now()->subDays(30)->toDateString())
            ->orderBy('check_in')
            ->get();

        foreach ($bookings as $booking) {
            $checkInStr = $booking->check_in instanceof \DateTimeInterface
                ? $booking->check_in->format('Y-m-d')
                : substr((string) $booking->check_in, 0, 10);
            $checkOutStr = $booking->check_out instanceof \DateTimeInterface
                ? $booking->check_out->format('Y-m-d')
                : substr((string) $booking->check_out, 0, 10);

            $vevent = $vcalendar->add('VEVENT', [
                'UID' => "{$booking->booking_code}@theboecottage.com",
                'SUMMARY' => "Reserved - {$booking->booking_code}",
                'DESCRIPTION' => "Room: {$room->name} | Source: {$booking->source} | Status: {$booking->status}",
                'STATUS' => 'CONFIRMED',
            ]);

            $vevent->add('DTSTART', new DateTimeImmutable($checkInStr), ['VALUE' => 'DATE']);
            $vevent->add('DTEND', new DateTimeImmutable($checkOutStr), ['VALUE' => 'DATE']);
        }

        if ($room->status === 'MAINTENANCE') {
            $maintStart = now()->format('Y-m-d');
            $maintEnd = now()->addDays(30)->format('Y-m-d');

            $maintEvent = $vcalendar->add('VEVENT', [
                'UID' => "MAINTENANCE-{$room->slug}@theboecottage.com",
                'SUMMARY' => "Reserved - Maintenance ({$room->name})",
                'DESCRIPTION' => "Unit under scheduled maintenance",
                'STATUS' => 'CONFIRMED',
            ]);

            $maintEvent->add('DTSTART', new DateTimeImmutable($maintStart), ['VALUE' => 'DATE']);
            $maintEvent->add('DTEND', new DateTimeImmutable($maintEnd), ['VALUE' => 'DATE']);
        }

        $icsOutput = $vcalendar->serialize();

        return response($icsOutput, 200, [
            'Content-Type' => 'text/calendar; charset=utf-8',
            'Content-Disposition' => "attachment; filename=\"{$room->slug}.ics\"",
            'Cache-Control' => 'no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate',
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Store or update an inbound OTA iCal link.
     * POST /admin/ical-links
     */
    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'id' => 'nullable|exists:ical_links,id',
            'room_id' => 'required|exists:rooms,id',
            'platform' => 'required|in:airbnb,tiket_com,booking_com,agoda,other',
            'url' => 'required|url|max:2000',
            'is_active' => 'nullable|boolean',
        ]);

        $room = Room::findOrFail($validated['room_id']);

        if (!empty($validated['id'])) {
            $icalLink = IcalLink::findOrFail($validated['id']);
            $icalLink->update([
                'room_id' => $room->id,
                'platform' => $validated['platform'],
                'url' => $validated['url'],
                'export_token' => $icalLink->export_token ?: $room->export_token,
                'is_active' => $validated['is_active'] ?? true,
            ]);
        } else {
            $icalLink = IcalLink::updateOrCreate(
                [
                    'room_id' => $room->id,
                    'platform' => $validated['platform'],
                    'url' => $validated['url'],
                ],
                [
                    'export_token' => $room->export_token,
                    'is_active' => $validated['is_active'] ?? true,
                ]
            );
        }

        return response()->json([
            'ical_link' => $icalLink->fresh('room'),
            'message' => 'OTA iCal channel link saved successfully.',
        ], 201);
    }

    /**
     * Delete an inbound OTA iCal link.
     * DELETE /admin/ical-links/{icalLink}
     */
    public function destroy(IcalLink $icalLink)
    {
        $icalLink->delete();

        return response()->json([
            'message' => 'OTA iCal channel link removed.',
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Trigger immediate sync of all active OTA iCal links.
     * POST /admin/ical-links/sync
     */
    public function syncNow(IcalSyncService $syncService)
    {
        $summary = $syncService->syncAll();

        return response()->json([
            'summary' => $summary,
            'message' => "OTA Sync Complete: {$summary['imported']} imported, {$summary['updated']} updated, {$summary['cancelled']} cancelled ({$summary['errors']} errors).",
        ]);
    }
}


==> app/Http/Controllers/RoomController.php <==
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Review;
use App\Models\Room;
use App\Models\SeasonalRate;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Inertia\Inertia;

class RoomController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $rooms = Room::with('images')->latest()->get();
        return Inertia::render('Admin/AdminDashboard', ['rooms' => $rooms]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'description' => 'nullable|string',
            'price_per_night' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'capacity' => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'status' => 'nullable|in:AVAILABLE,OCCUPIED,MAINTENANCE',
            'size' => 'nullable|string|max:100',
        ]);

        $validated['slug'] = Str::slug($request->name) . '-' . Str::random(5);

        $room = Room::create($validated);

        if ($request->expectsJson()) {
            return response()->json(['room' => $room->fresh('images'), 'message' => 'Kamar berhasil ditambahkan!'], 201);
        }

        return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Kamar berhasil ditambahkan!');
    }

    public function update(Request $request, Room $room)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'description' => 'nullable|string',
            'price_per_night' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'capacity' => 'nullable|integer|min:1',
            'status' => 'nullable|in:AVAILABLE,OCCUPIED,MAINTENANCE',
            'size' => 'nullable|string|max:100',
        ]);

        $room->update(array_filter($validated, fn ($v) => !is_null($v)));

        if ($request->expectsJson()) {
            return response()->json(['room' => $room->fresh('images'), 'message' => 'Kamar berhasil diperbarui!']);
        }

        return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Kamar berhasil diperbarui!');
    }

    public function updateStatus(Request $request, Room $room)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'status' => 'required|in:AVAILABLE,OCCUPIED,MAINTENANCE',
        ]);

        $room->update(['status' => $validated['status']]);

        return response()->json([
            'room' => $room->fresh('images'),
            'message' => 'Status kamar berhasil diperbarui!',
        ]);
    }

    public function storeSeasonalRate(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'rule_name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'room_id' => 'nullable|exists:rooms,id',
            'start_date' => 'required|date',
            'end_date' => 'required|date|after_or_equal:start_date',
            'rate_adjustment_percent' => 'required|numeric|min:-90|max:500',
        ]);

        $rule = SeasonalRate::create([
            ...$validated,
            'is_active' => true,
        ]);

        return response()->json([
            'seasonal_rate' => $rule->fresh('room'),
            'message' => 'Seasonal rate rule saved successfully.',
        ], 201);
    }

    public function storeReview(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'guest_name' => 'nullable|string|max:255',
            'rating' => 'required|integer|min:1|max:5',
            'comment' => 'required|string|max:2000',
        ]);

        $review = Review::create([
            'guest_name' => !empty($validated['guest_name']) ? $validated['guest_name'] : 'Verified Guest',
            'rating' => $validated['rating'],
            'comment' => $validated['comment'],
            'is_approved' => true,
        ]);

        return response()->json([
            'review' => $review,
            'message' => 'Thank you for sharing your review!',
        ], 201);
    }

    public function destroy(Room $room)
    {
        $room->delete();
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Kamar berhasil dihapus!');
    }
}

==> app/Http/Middleware/HandleInertiaRequests.php <==
<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Inertia\Middleware;

class HandleInertiaRequests extends Middleware
{
    /**
     * The root template that's loaded on the first page visit.
     *
     * @see https://inertiajs.com/server-side-setup#root-template
     *
     * @var string
     */
    protected $rootView = 'app';

    /**
     * Determines the current asset version.
     *
     * @see https://inertiajs.com/asset-versioning
     */
    public function version(Request $request): ?string
    {
        return parent::version($request);
    }

    /**
     * Define the props that are shared by default.
     *
     * @see https://inertiajs.com/shared-data
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function share(Request $request): array
    {
        return [
            ...parent::share($request),
            'auth' => [
                'user' => $request->user() ? [
                    'id' => $request->user()->id,
                    'name' => $request->user()->name,
                    'email' => $request->user()->email,
                ] : null,
            ],
            'midtrans' => [
                'client_key' => config('services.midtrans.client_key'),
                'is_production' => (bool) config('services.midtrans.is_production', false),
            ],
        ];
    }
}

==> app/Models/Room.php <==
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;

class Room extends Model
{
    protected $fillable = [
        'name',
        'slug',
        'description',
        'price_per_night',
        'capacity',
        'status',
        'size',
        'amenities',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'price_per_night' => 'decimal:2',
        'capacity' => 'integer',
        'amenities' => 'array',
    ];

    protected $appends = [
        'export_token',
        'export_url',
    ];

    public function getExportTokenAttribute(): string
    {
        return substr(hash_hmac('sha256', 'ical-export-' . $this->slug, (string) config('app.key', 'the-boe-cottage')), 0, 24);
    }

    public function getExportUrlAttribute(): string
    {
        return url("/ical/export/{$this->slug}.ics?token={$this->export_token}");
    }

    public function images(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(RoomImage::class);
    }

    public function bookings(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Booking::class);
    }

    public function icalLinks(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(IcalLink::class);
    }

    public function seasonalRates(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(SeasonalRate::class);
    }
}

==> app/Services/IcalSyncService.php <==
<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\Booking;
use App\Models\IcalLink;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Sabre\VObject\Reader;
use Throwable;

class IcalSyncService
{
    /**
     * Sync all active iCal links and return an aggregated summary.
     *
     * @return array{imported: int, updated: int, cancelled: int, errors: int}
     */
    public function syncAll(): array
    {
        $links = IcalLink::where('is_active', true)->get();

        $totals = [
            'imported' => 0,
            'updated' => 0,
            'cancelled' => 0,
            'errors' => 0,
        ];

        foreach ($links as $link) {
            $result = $this->sync($link);
            $totals['imported'] += $result['imported'];
            $totals['updated'] += $result['updated'];
            $totals['cancelled'] += $result['cancelled'];
            $totals['errors'] += $result['errors'];
        }

        return $totals;
    }

    /**
     * Sync a single iCal link from Airbnb, Tiket.com, Booking.com, etc.
     *
     * @return array{imported: int, updated: int, cancelled: int, errors: int}
     */
    public function sync(IcalLink $icalLink): array
    {
        $summary = [
            'imported' => 0,
            'updated' => 0,
            'cancelled' => 0,
            'errors' => 0,
        ];

        try {
            $response = Http::timeout(15)
                ->withUserAgent('Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; TheBoeCottageChannelManager/1.0)')
                ->get($icalLink->url);

            if (!$response->successful() || empty(trim($response->body()))) {
                $summary['errors']++;
                return $summary;
            }

            $icsContent = $response->body();
            $vcalendar = Reader::read($icsContent);
        } catch (Throwable $e) {
            Log::warning('Failed to fetch or parse iCal feed', [
                'ical_link_id' => $icalLink->id,
                'url' => $icalLink->url,
                'error' => $e->getMessage(),
            ]);
            $summary['errors']++;
            return $summary;
        }

        $source = $this->mapPlatformToSource($icalLink->platform);
        $seenUids = [];

        if (isset($vcalendar->VEVENT)) {
            foreach ($vcalendar->VEVENT as $event) {
                try {
                    if (!isset($event->DTSTART) || !isset($event->DTEND)) {
                        continue;
                    }

                    $eventStatus = isset($event->STATUS) ? strtoupper(trim((string) $event->STATUS)) : 'CONFIRMED';
                    if ($eventStatus === 'CANCELLED') {
                        continue;
                    }

                    $startDate = $event->DTSTART->getDateTime()->format('Y-m-d');
                    $endDate = $event->DTEND->getDateTime()->format('Y-m-d');

                    if ($endDate <= $startDate) {
                        continue;
                    }

                    $rawUid = isset($event->UID) ? trim((string) $event->UID) : '';
                    $externalUid = $rawUid !== ''
                        ? $rawUid
                        : md5($icalLink->id . '-' . $startDate . '-' . $endDate);

                    $seenUids[] = $externalUid;

                    $eventSummary = isset($event->SUMMARY) ? trim((string) $event->SUMMARY) : '';
                    $platformLabel = $this->formatPlatformLabel($icalLink->platform);
                    $guestName = ($eventSummary !== '' && !in_array(strtolower($eventSummary), ['reserved', 'not available', 'closed - not available'], true))
                        ? "{$eventSummary} ({$platformLabel})"
                        : "Reserved ({$platformLabel})";

                    $existing = Booking::where('room_id', $icalLink->room_id)
                        ->where('source', $source)
                        ->where('external_uid', $externalUid)
                        ->first();

                    $bookingCode = $existing?->booking_code;
                    if (!$bookingCode) {
                        do {
                            $bookingCode = 'OTA-' . Str::upper(Str::random(6));
                        } while (Booking::where('booking_code', $bookingCode)->exists());
                    }

                    $booking = Booking::updateOrCreate(
                        [
                            'room_id' => $icalLink->room_id,
                            'source' => $source,
                            'external_uid' => $externalUid,
                        ],
                        [
                            'booking_code' => $bookingCode,
                            'guest_name' => $guestName,
                            'check_in' => $startDate,
                            'check_out' => $endDate,
                            'total_price' => $existing?->total_price ?? 0,
                            'status' => 'confirmed',
                            'payment_status' => 'paid',
                            'payment_type' => $platformLabel,
                            'paid_at' => $existing?->paid_at ?? now(),
                        ]
                    );

                    if ($booking->wasRecentlyCreated) {
                        $summary['imported']++;
                    } else {
                        $summary['updated']++;
                    }
                } catch (Throwable $e) {
                    Log::warning('Error processing individual VEVENT during iCal sync', [
                        'ical_link_id' => $icalLink->id,
                        'error' => $e->getMessage(),
                    ]);
                    $summary['errors']++;
                }
            }
        }

        // Handle OTA Cancellations for future bookings from this source & room whose external_uid disappeared
        $today = now()->toDateString();
        $cancelQuery = Booking::where('room_id', $icalLink->room_id)
            ->where('source', $source)
            ->where('check_out', '>=', $today)
            ->whereNotNull('external_uid')
            ->where('status', '!=', 'cancelled');

        if (!empty($seenUids)) {
            $cancelQuery->whereNotIn('external_uid', $seenUids);
        }

        $cancelledCount = $cancelQuery->update(['status' => 'cancelled']);
        $summary['cancelled'] += $cancelledCount;

        $icalLink->update(['last_synced_at' => now()]);

        return $summary;
    }

    public function mapPlatformToSource(string $platform): string
    {
        return match ($platform) {
            'airbnb' => 'airbnb',
            'tiket_com' => 'tiket_com',
            'booking_com' => 'booking_com',
            'agoda' => 'agoda',
            default => 'manual',
        };
    }

    private function formatPlatformLabel(string $platform): string
    {
        return match ($platform) {
            'airbnb' => 'Airbnb',
            'tiket_com' => 'Tiket.com',
            'booking_com' => 'Booking.com',
            'agoda' => 'Agoda',
            default => 'OTA',
        };
    }
}

==> app/Services/MidtransService.php <==
<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\Booking;
use App\Models\Room;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use RuntimeException;

class MidtransService
{
    /**
     * Generate a Midtrans Snap token for a booking.
     */
    public function createSnapToken(Booking $booking, Room $room, array $itemDetails = []): string
    {
        $serverKey = (string) config('services.midtrans.server_key');
        $isProduction = (bool) config('services.midtrans.is_production', false);

        if (empty($serverKey)) {
            Log::warning('MIDTRANS_SERVER_KEY is not configured. Returning local development fallback snap_token.', [
                'booking_code' => $booking->booking_code,
            ]);

            return 'MOCK-SNAP-' . $booking->booking_code;
        }

        $endpoint = $isProduction
            ? 'https://app.midtrans.com/snap/v1/transactions'
            : 'https://app.sandbox.midtrans.com/snap/v1/transactions';

        $grossAmount = (int) round((float) $booking->total_price);

        $payload = [
            'transaction_details' => [
                'order_id' => $booking->booking_code,
                'gross_amount' => $grossAmount,
            ],
            'customer_details' => [
                'first_name' => $booking->guest_name,
                'email' => $booking->guest_email,
                'phone' => $booking->guest_phone ?? '',
            ],
            'credit_card' => [
                'secure' => (bool) config('services.midtrans.is_3ds', true),
            ],
        ];

        if (!empty($itemDetails)) {
            $payload['item_details'] = $itemDetails;
        }

        $authHeader = 'Basic ' . base64_encode($serverKey . ':');

        $response = Http::timeout(20)
            ->withHeaders([
                'Accept' => 'application/json',
                'Content-Type' => 'application/json',
                'Authorization' => $authHeader,
            ])
            ->post($endpoint, $payload);

        if (!$response->successful() || !$response->json('token')) {
            Log::error('Midtrans Snap API error', [
                'status' => $response->status(),
                'body' => $response->json() ?? $response->body(),
            ]);

            throw new RuntimeException(
                $response->json('error_messages.0') ?? 'Failed to initialize Midtrans Snap payment.'
            );
        }

        return (string) $response->json('token');
    }

    /**
     * Verify Midtrans webhook SHA-512 signature.
     */
    public function verifySignature(string $orderId, string $statusCode, string $grossAmount, string $signatureKey): bool
    {
        $serverKey = (string) config('services.midtrans.server_key');
        $expectedSignature = hash('sha512', $orderId . $statusCode . $grossAmount . $serverKey);

        return hash_equals($expectedSignature, $signatureKey);
    }
}

<?php

use App\Models\User;

return [

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Authentication Defaults
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option defines the default authentication "guard" and password
    | reset "broker" for your application. You may change these values
    | as required, but they're a perfect start for most applications.
    |
    */

    'defaults' => [
        'guard' => env('AUTH_GUARD', 'web'),
        'passwords' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_BROKER', 'users'),
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Authentication Guards
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Next, you may define every authentication guard for your application.
    | Of course, a great default configuration has been defined for you
    | which utilizes session storage plus the Eloquent user provider.
    |
    | All authentication guards have a user provider, which defines how the
    | users are actually retrieved out of your database or other storage
    | system used by the application. Typically, Eloquent is utilized.
    |
    | Supported: "session"
    |
    */

    'guards' => [
        'web' => [
            'driver' => 'session',
            'provider' => 'users',
        ],
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | User Providers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | All authentication guards have a user provider, which defines how the
    | users are actually retrieved out of your database or other storage
    | system used by the application. Typically, Eloquent is utilized.
    |
    | If you have multiple user tables or models you may configure multiple
    | providers to represent the model / table. These providers may then
    | be assigned to any extra authentication guards you have defined.
    |
    | Supported: "database", "eloquent"
    |
    */

    'providers' => [
        'users' => [
            'driver' => 'eloquent',
            'model' => env('AUTH_MODEL', User::class),
        ],

        // 'users' => [
        //     'driver' => 'database',
        //     'table' => 'users',
        // ],
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Resetting Passwords
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | These configuration options specify the behavior of Laravel's password
    | reset functionality, including the table utilized for token storage
    | and the user provider that is invoked to actually retrieve users.
    |
    | The expiry time is the number of minutes that each reset token will be
    | considered valid. This security feature keeps tokens short-lived so
    | they have less time to be guessed. You may change this as needed.
    |
    | The throttle setting is the number of seconds a user must wait before
    | generating more password reset tokens. This prevents the user from
    | quickly generating a very large amount of password reset tokens.
    |
    */

    'passwords' => [
        'users' => [
            'provider' => 'users',
            'table' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_TABLE', 'password_reset_tokens'),
            'expire' => 60,
            'throttle' => 60,
        ],
    ],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Password Confirmation Timeout
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Here you may define the number of seconds before a password confirmation
    | window expires and users are asked to re-enter their password via the
    | confirmation screen. By default, the timeout lasts for three hours.
    |
    */

    'password_timeout' => env('AUTH_PASSWORD_TIMEOUT', 10800),

];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Default Log Channel

==> config/session.php <==
<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Str;

return [

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Default Session Driver
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option determines the default session driver that is utilized for
    | incoming requests. Laravel supports a variety of storage options to
    | persist session data. Database storage is a great default choice.
    |
    | Supported: "file", "cookie", "database", "memcached",
    |            "redis", "dynamodb", "array"
    |
    */

    'driver' => env('SESSION_DRIVER', 'database'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Lifetime
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Here you may specify the number of minutes that you wish the session
    | to be allowed to remain idle before it expires. If you want them
    | to expire immediately when the browser is closed then you may
    | indicate that via the expire_on_close configuration option.
    |
    */

    'lifetime' => (int) env('SESSION_LIFETIME', 120),

    'expire_on_close' => env('SESSION_EXPIRE_ON_CLOSE', false),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Encryption
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option allows you to easily specify that all of your session data
    | should be encrypted before it's stored. All encryption is performed
    | automatically by Laravel and you may use the session like normal.
    |
    */

    'encrypt' => env('SESSION_ENCRYPT', false),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session File Location
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When utilizing the "file" session driver, the session files are placed
    | on disk. The default storage location is defined here; however, you
    | are free to provide another location where they should be stored.
    |
    */

    'files' => storage_path('framework/sessions'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Database Connection
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When using the "database" or "redis" session drivers, you may specify a
    | connection that should be used to manage these sessions. This should
    | correspond to a connection in your database configuration options.
    |
    */

    'connection' => env('SESSION_CONNECTION'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Database Table
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When using the "database" session driver, you may specify the table to
    | be used to store sessions. Of course, a sensible default is defined
    | for you; however, you're welcome to change this to another table.
    |
    */

    'table' => env('SESSION_TABLE', 'sessions'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cache Store
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | When using one of the framework's cache driven session backends, you may
    | define the cache store which should be used to store the session data
    | between requests. This must match one of your defined cache stores.
    |
    | Affects: "dynamodb", "memcached", "redis"
    |
    */

    'store' => env('SESSION_STORE'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Sweeping Lottery
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Some session drivers must manually sweep their storage location to get
    | rid of old sessions from storage. Here are the chances that it will
    | happen on a given request. By default, the odds are 2 out of 100.
    |
    */

    'lottery' => [2, 100],

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cookie Name
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Here you may change the name of the session cookie that is created by
    | the framework. Typically, you should not need to change this value
    | since doing so does not grant a meaningful security improvement.
    |
    */

    'cookie' => env(
        'SESSION_COOKIE',
        Str::slug((string) env('APP_NAME', 'laravel')).'-session'
    ),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cookie Path
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | The session cookie path determines the path for which the cookie will
    | be regarded as available. Typically, this will be the root path of
    | your application, but you're free to change this when necessary.
    |
    */

    'path' => env('SESSION_PATH', '/'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Cookie Domain
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This value determines the domain and subdomains the session cookie is
    | available to. By default, the cookie will be available to the root
    | domain without subdomains. Typically, this shouldn't be changed.
    |
    */

    'domain' => env('SESSION_DOMAIN'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | HTTPS Only Cookies
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | By setting this option to true, session cookies will only be sent back
    | to the server if the browser has a HTTPS connection. This will keep
    | the cookie from being sent to you when it can't be done securely.
    |
    */

    'secure' => env('SESSION_SECURE_COOKIE'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | HTTP Access Only
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Setting this value to true will prevent JavaScript from accessing the
    | value of the cookie and the cookie will only be accessible through
    | the HTTP protocol. It's unlikely you should disable this option.
    |
    */

    'http_only' => env('SESSION_HTTP_ONLY', true),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Same-Site Cookies
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This option determines how your cookies behave when cross-site requests
    | take place, and can be used to mitigate CSRF attacks. By default, we
    | will set this value to "lax" to permit secure cross-site requests.
    |
    | See: https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Headers/Set-Cookie#samesitesamesite-value
    |
    | Supported: "lax", "strict", "none", null
    |
    */

    'same_site' => env('SESSION_SAME_SITE', 'lax'),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Partitioned Cookies
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Setting this value to true will tie the cookie to the top-level site for
    | a cross-site context. Partitioned cookies are accepted by the browser
    | when flagged "secure" and the Same-Site attribute is set to "none".
    |
    */

    'partitioned' => env('SESSION_PARTITIONED_COOKIE', false),

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Session Serialization
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This value controls the serialization strategy for session data, which
    | is JSON by default. Setting this to "php" allows the storage of PHP
    | objects in the session but can make an application vulnerable to
    | "gadget chain" serialization attacks if the APP_KEY is leaked.
    |
    | Supported: "json", "php"
    |
    */

    'serialization' => 'json',

];

==> database/factories/UserFactory.php <==
<?php

namespace Database\Factories;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

/**
 * @extends Factory<User>
 */
class UserFactory extends Factory
{
    /**
     * The current password being used by the factory.
     */
    protected static ?string $password;

    /**
     * Define the model's default state.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function definition(): array
    {
        return [
            'name' => fake()->name(),
            'email' => fake()->unique()->safeEmail(),
            'email_verified_at' => now(),
            'password' => static::$password ??= Hash::make('password'),
            'remember_token' => Str::random(10),
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Indicate that the model's email address should be unverified.
     */
    public function unverified(): static
    {
        return $this->state(fn (array $attributes) => [
            'email_verified_at' => null,
        ]);
    }
}

            $table->integer('pending_jobs');
            $table->integer('failed_jobs');
            $table->longText('failed_job_ids');
            $table->mediumText('options')->nullable();
            $table->integer('cancelled_at')->nullable();
            $table->integer('created_at');
            $table->integer('finished_at')->nullable();
        });

        Schema::create('failed_jobs', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->id();
            $table->string('uuid')->unique();
            $table->string('connection');
            $table->string('queue');
            $table->longText('payload');
            $table->longText('exception');
            $table->timestamp('failed_at')->useCurrent();

            $table->index(['connection', 'queue', 'failed_at']);
        });
    }

        Schema::dropIfExists('job_batches');

==> resources/js/Components/Footer.jsx <==
import { router } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import RevealOnScroll from './RevealOnScroll';

function ContactIcon({ type }) {
    const paths = {
        email: <><rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="14" rx="2" /><path d="m4 7 8 6 8-6" /></>,
        phone: <path d="M7 3H5a2 2 0 0 0-2 2c0 8.837 7.163 16 16 16a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-2l-5-2-2 3a14 14 0 0 1-6-6l3-2-2-5H7z" />,
        location: <><path d="M20 10c0 5-8 11-8 11S4 15 4 10a8 8 0 1 1 16 0Z" /><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="2.5" /></>,
    };

    return (
        <svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-[#648C7B]">
            {paths[type]}
        </svg>
    );
}

export default function Footer() {
    const [reviewForm, setReviewForm] = useState({ guest_name: '', rating: 0, review: '' });
    const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
    const [statusMessage, setStatusMessage] = useState('');

    async function handleReviewSubmit(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (reviewForm.rating === 0 || !reviewForm.review.trim()) {
            setStatusMessage('Please select a star rating and enter your review before submitting.');
            return;
        }

        setSubmitting(true);
        setStatusMessage('');

        try {
            await window.axios.post('/api/reviews', {
                guest_name: reviewForm.guest_name.trim() || 'Verified Guest',
                rating: reviewForm.rating,
                comment: reviewForm.review.trim(),
            });
            setReviewForm({ guest_name: '', rating: 0, review: '' });
            setStatusMessage('Thank you! Your review has been published.');
            router.reload({ only: ['reviews'] });
        } catch (error) {
            setStatusMessage(error.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to submit review. Please try again.');
        } finally {
            setSubmitting(false);
        }
    }

    function handleUnavailablePolicy(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        console.info('Legal policy content is not available yet.');
    }

    return (
        <footer>
            <section className="bg-[#1E322A] px-6 py-20 text-center sm:py-24">

import { router } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

function FieldIcon({ type }) {
    const paths = {
        email: <><rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="14" rx="2" /><path d="m4 7 8 6 8-6" /></>,
        lock: <><rect x="4" y="10" width="16" height="11" rx="2" /><path d="M8 10V7a4 4 0 0 1 8 0v3m-4 4v3" /></>,
        eye: <><path d="M2 12s3.5-6 10-6 10 6 10 6-3.5 6-10 6S2 12 2 12Z" /><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2.5" /></>,
        eyeOff: <><path d="m3 3 18 18M10.6 6.2A10.9 10.9 0 0 1 12 6c6.5 0 10 6 10 6a16 16 0 0 1-3.1 3.6M6.2 6.2C3.5 7.8 2 12 2 12s3.5 6 10 6a10.9 10.9 0 0 0 3.1-.5" /><path d="M9.9 9.9a3 3 0 0 0 4.2 4.2" /></>,
    };

    return (
        <svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.6" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" className="h-4 w-4">
            {paths[type]}
        </svg>
    );
}

function BrandMark() {
    return (
        <div aria-label="The Boe Cottage" className="mx-auto flex h-10 w-10 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#F8F1E8] text-[#AE8A66]">
            <span className="font-serif text-[6px] italic leading-[6px]">The</span>
            <span className="font-serif text-[7px] leading-[8px] tracking-[0.05em]">BOE</span>
            <span className="font-serif text-[6px] leading-[6px] tracking-[0.02em]">COTTAGE</span>
        </div>
    );
}

export default function LoginModal({ isOpen, onClose }) {
    const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ email: '', password: '', remember: false });
    const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

    function updateFormData(event) {
        const { name, value, type, checked } = event.target;
        setFormData((current) => ({ ...current, [name]: type === 'checkbox' ? checked : value }));
    }

    async function handleLoginSubmit(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        setErrorMessage('');
        setLoading(true);

        try {
            const response = await window.axios.post('/login', formData);
            onClose();
            router.visit(response.data?.redirect || '/admin-dashboard');
        } catch (error) {
            const msg = error.response?.data?.errors?.email?.[0]
                || error.response?.data?.message
                || 'Invalid credentials. Please try again.';
            setErrorMessage(msg);
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    }

    function handleUnsupportedAction(action) {
        setErrorMessage(`${action} is managed by the Lead Desk Administrator.`);
    }

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isOpen) return undefined;

        const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';

        const handleKeyDown = (event) => {
            if (event.key === 'Escape') onClose();
        };

        window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
        return () => {
            document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
            window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
        };
    }, [isOpen, onClose]);

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        <div
            className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center overflow-y-auto bg-black/50 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm sm:p-6"
            onMouseDown={(event) => {
                if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose();
            }}
        >
            <section
                role="dialog"
                aria-modal="true"
                aria-labelledby="login-modal-title"
                className="relative my-auto w-full max-w-[390px] overflow-hidden rounded-[3px] border border-[#E7E4DE] bg-[#FBF9F4] shadow-[0_24px_80px_rgba(0,0,0,0.24)]"
            >
                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={onClose}
                    aria-label="Close login dialog"
                    className="absolute right-3 top-3 z-10 flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[#666A64] transition-colors hover:bg-[#F1ECE0] hover:text-[#1E322A] focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-[#346B55]"
                >
                    <svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7" strokeLinecap="round" className="h-4 w-4">
                        <path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18" />
                    </svg>
                </button>

                <div className="px-7 pb-7 pt-9 sm:px-9 sm:pt-10">
                    <BrandMark />

                    <div className="mt-5">
                        <header className="mb-6 text-center">
                            <h2 id="login-modal-title" className="font-serif text-[21px] font-normal leading-tight text-[#1E322A]">
                                Hello! Ready to Manage Your Stay?
                            </h2>
                            <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-[260px] font-sans text-xs leading-relaxed text-[#666A64]">
                                Sign in to access the staff ledger, manage reservations, and configure cottage availability.
                            </p>
                        </header>

                        {errorMessage && (
                            <div role="alert" className="mb-4 border border-[#AE5535]/40 bg-[#FADBD1]/50 px-3 py-2 font-sans text-[11px] text-[#8E3D24]">
                                {errorMessage}
                            </div>
                        )}

                        <form onSubmit={handleLoginSubmit} className="space-y-4">
                            <div>
                                <label htmlFor="guest-email" className="mb-1 block font-sans text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-[#303832]">
                                    Email Address
                                </label>
                                <div className="flex h-10 items-center gap-2.5 bg-[#F3F2ED] px-3 text-[#858A83]">
                                    <FieldIcon type="email" />
                                    <input required id="guest-email" name="email" type="email" value={formData.email} onChange={updateFormData} autoComplete="email" placeholder="admin@theboecottage.com" className="h-full min-w-0 flex-1 bg-transparent font-sans text-xs text-[#303832] outline-none placeholder:text-[#858A83]" />
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div>
                                <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                                    <label htmlFor="guest-password" className="font-sans text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-[#303832]">
                                        Password
                                    </label>
                                    <button type="button" onClick={() => handleUnsupportedAction('Password recovery')} className="font-sans text-[9px] text-[#AE6548] hover:underline focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-[#346B55]">
                                        Forgot Password?
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                                <div className="flex h-10 items-center gap-2.5 bg-[#F3F2ED] px-3 text-[#858A83]">
                                    <FieldIcon type="lock" />
                                    <input required id="guest-password" name="password" value={formData.password} onChange={updateFormData} type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'} autoComplete="current-password" placeholder="••••••••••" className="h-full min-w-0 flex-1 bg-transparent font-sans text-xs text-[#303832] outline-none placeholder:text-[#B2B4AE]" />
                                    <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)} aria-label={showPassword ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'} className="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center transition-colors hover:text-[#1E322A] focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-[#346B55]">
                                        <FieldIcon type={showPassword ? 'eyeOff' : 'eye'} />
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-2 font-sans text-[11px] leading-5 text-[#666A64]">
                                <input name="remember" type="checkbox" checked={formData.remember} onChange={updateFormData} className="mt-1 h-3 w-3 accent-[#1E322A]" />
                                Remember on this terminal
                            </label>

                            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="flex h-10 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-[#071711] font-sans text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.1em] text-white transition-colors hover:bg-[#1E322A] disabled:opacity-60 focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-[#346B55]">
                                {loading ? 'SIGNING IN...' : 'SIGN IN'} <span aria-hidden="true">→</span>
                            </button>
                        </form>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </section>
        </div>
    );
}

==> resources/js/Components/RevealOnScroll.jsx <==
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';

export default function RevealOnScroll({
    as: Element = 'div',
    children,
    className = '',
    direction = 'up',
    delay = 0,
    style = {},
}) {
    const elementRef = useRef(null);
    const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        const element = elementRef.current;
        if (!element) return undefined;

        if (
            window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches
            || !('IntersectionObserver' in window)
        ) {
            setIsVisible(true);
            return undefined;
        }

        let observer;
        const reveal = () => {
            setIsVisible(true);
            observer?.disconnect();
            window.removeEventListener('scroll', checkVisibility);
            window.removeEventListener('resize', checkVisibility);
        };
        const checkVisibility = () => {
            const { top, bottom } = element.getBoundingClientRect();
            if (top < window.innerHeight * 1.05 && bottom > 0) {
                reveal();
            }
        };

        observer = new IntersectionObserver(([entry]) => {
            if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                reveal();
            }
        }, { threshold: 0.12, rootMargin: '0px 0px -5% 0px' });

        observer.observe(element);
        window.addEventListener('scroll', checkVisibility, { passive: true });
        window.addEventListener('resize', checkVisibility);
        window.requestAnimationFrame(checkVisibility);

        return () => {
            observer.disconnect();
            window.removeEventListener('scroll', checkVisibility);
            window.removeEventListener('resize', checkVisibility);
        };
    }, []);

    return (
        <Element
            ref={elementRef}
            className={`reveal-on-scroll reveal-${direction}${isVisible ? ' is-visible' : ''} ${className}`}
            style={{ ...style, '--reveal-delay': `${delay}ms` }}
        >
            {children}
        </Element>
    );
}

        guest_name: 'Risma Ayu',

    },
    {
        id: 3,
        comment: 'Desain kayu jati dan batu alamnya begitu hangat di tengah udara dingin Dieng. Pelayanan yang sangat tenang dan berkelas.',
        guest_name: 'Dr. Hendra Kusuma',
        rating: 5,
        created_at: '2026-10-18',
    },
];

function formatReviewDate(dateString) {
    if (!dateString) return 'VERIFIED STAY';
    const parsed = new Date(dateString);
    if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) return String(dateString);
    return parsed.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' }).toUpperCase();
}

export default function ReviewSection({ reviews = [] }) {
    const items = reviews.length > 0 ? reviews : defaultReviews;

    return (
        <section id="testimonials" className="bg-[#1E322A] px-6 py-20 sm:py-24">
            <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl">
                <header className="mb-12 text-center sm:mb-16">
                    <h2 className="font-serif text-4xl font-normal leading-tight tracking-[-0.025em] text-[#F1ECE0] sm:text-5xl">
                        Impressions Left on Faded Paper
                    </h2>
                    <p className="mx-auto mt-4 max-w-xl font-sans text-sm leading-relaxed text-gray-300 sm:text-base">
                        Real stories and memories from guests who found peace in the embrace of nature
