import { Head, Link, usePage } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import AboutSection from '../Components/AboutSection';
import Footer from '../Components/Footer';
import LoginModal from '../Components/LoginModal';
import LocationSection from '../Components/LocationSection';
import ReviewSection from '../Components/ReviewSection';
import RoomSection from '../Components/RoomSection';

function CalendarIcon() {
    return <svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none" className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-[#AE8A66]" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5"><rect x="3.25" y="4.5" width="13.5" height="12" rx="1.5" /><path d="M6.5 2.75v3.5m7-3.5v3.5M3.5 8h13" /></svg>;
}

function Navbar({ onOpenLogin, user }) {
    const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
    const navLinks = [
        { label: 'About Us', href: '#about' },
        { label: 'The Cottages', href: '#cottages' },
        { label: 'Location', href: '#location' },
        { label: 'Testimonials', href: '#testimonials' },
    ];

    return (
        <nav className="sticky top-0 z-50 h-[76px] bg-[#F1ECE0]">
            <div className="mx-auto flex h-full max-w-[1440px] items-center justify-between px-6 sm:px-10 lg:px-[4.25%]">
                <a href="/" aria-label="The Boe Cottage home" className="flex w-[112px] shrink-0 flex-col items-center text-[#AE8A66] no-underline">
                    <span className="font-serif text-[9px] italic leading-[10px]">The</span>
                    <span className="font-serif text-[15px] leading-[15px] tracking-[0.08em]">BOE</span>
                    <span className="font-serif text-[18px] leading-[18px] tracking-[0.03em]">COTTAGE</span>
                    <span className="font-sans text-[6px] font-medium leading-[9px] tracking-[0.48em]">DIENG</span>
                </a>

                <div className="hidden items-center gap-7 md:flex lg:gap-9">
                    {navLinks.map(({ label, href }) => (
                        <a key={label} href={href} className="font-sans text-[15px] font-medium text-[#424641] transition-colors hover:text-[#AE8A66]">
                            {label}
                        </a>
                    ))}
                </div>

                <div className="flex w-[140px] items-center justify-end gap-2">
                    {user ? (
                        <Link
                            href="/admin-dashboard"
                            className="inline-flex h-8 items-center justify-center rounded-[9px] bg-[#1E322A] px-3 font-sans text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.08em] text-white transition-colors hover:bg-[#346B55]"
                        >
                            DASHBOARD
                        </Link>
                    ) : (
                        <button type="button" onClick={onOpenLogin} aria-label="Open guest and staff login" className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-[9px] bg-[#1E322A] text-white transition-colors hover:bg-[#346B55]">
                            <svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
                                <circle cx="10" cy="6.5" r="2.25" />
                                <path d="M5.75 15.5v-1.25A2.25 2.25 0 0 1 8 12h4a2.25 2.25 0 0 1 2.25 2.25v1.25z" />
                            </svg>
                        </button>
                    )}
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setMenuOpen(!menuOpen)}
                        aria-label={menuOpen ? 'Close navigation menu' : 'Open navigation menu'}
                        aria-expanded={menuOpen}
                        className="ml-2 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-[#1E322A] md:hidden"
                    >
                        <svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" className="h-5 w-5" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7" strokeLinecap="round">
                            {menuOpen ? <path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18" /> : <path d="M4 7h16M4 12h16M4 17h16" />}
                        </svg>
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
            {menuOpen && (
                <div className="absolute inset-x-0 top-full flex flex-col gap-1 border-t border-[#AE8A66]/15 bg-[#F1ECE0] px-6 py-4 shadow-lg md:hidden">
                    {navLinks.map(({ label, href }) => (
                        <a key={label} href={href} onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)} className="rounded px-3 py-2.5 font-sans text-sm text-[#1E322A] hover:bg-white/60">
                            {label}
                        </a>
                    ))}
                    <Link href="/reservation" onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)} className="rounded px-3 py-2.5 font-sans text-sm text-[#1E322A] hover:bg-white/60 focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-[#346B55]">
                        Booking
                    </Link>
                </div>
            )}
        </nav>
    );
}

function HeroSection({ rooms = [] }) {
    const toInputDate = (date) => [
        date.getFullYear(),
        String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0'),
        String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0'),
    ].join('-');
    const initialArrival = new Date();
    initialArrival.setDate(initialArrival.getDate() + 1);
    const initialDeparture = new Date(initialArrival);
    initialDeparture.setDate(initialDeparture.getDate() + 2);

    const roomNames = rooms.length > 0 ? rooms.map((r) => r.name) : ['Lily Room', 'Daisy Room'];

    const [bookingData, setBookingData] = useState({
        checkIn: toInputDate(initialArrival),
        checkOut: toInputDate(initialDeparture),
        guests: '2',
        room: roomNames.includes('Lily Room') ? 'Lily Room' : roomNames[0],
    });
    const today = toInputDate(new Date());
    const formatDate = (value) => value
        ? new Date(`${value}T12:00:00`).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })
        : 'Select date';
    const bookingUrl = `/reservation?check_in=${bookingData.checkIn}&check_out=${bookingData.checkOut}&guests=${bookingData.guests}&room=${encodeURIComponent(bookingData.room)}`;
    const updateBookingData = (event) => {
        const { name, value } = event.target;
        setBookingData((current) => {
            if (name === 'checkIn' && current.checkOut <= value) {
                const nextDeparture = new Date(`${value}T12:00:00`);
                nextDeparture.setDate(nextDeparture.getDate() + 1);
                return { ...current, checkIn: value, checkOut: toInputDate(nextDeparture) };
            }

            if (name === 'checkOut' && value <= current.checkIn) {
                const nextDeparture = new Date(`${current.checkIn}T12:00:00`);
                nextDeparture.setDate(nextDeparture.getDate() + 1);
                return { ...current, checkOut: toInputDate(nextDeparture) };
            }

            return { ...current, [name]: value };
        });
    };

    return (
        <section id="hero" className="relative min-h-[calc(100svh-76px)] overflow-hidden bg-[#1E322A]">
            <img
                src="/images/cottage/hero/cottage-facade-main.png"
                alt="The Boe Cottage in the Dieng highlands"
                fetchPriority="high"
                className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover object-center"
            />
            <div aria-hidden="true" className="absolute inset-0 bg-[linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(13,35,36,0.25)_0%,rgba(13,35,36,0.32)_42%,rgba(13,35,36,0.58)_100%)]" />

            <div className="relative z-10 mx-auto flex min-h-[calc(100svh-76px)] flex-col items-center justify-center px-5 pb-[190px] pt-12 text-center sm:pb-[145px]">
                <h1 className="animate-title-reveal font-serif text-[clamp(1.25rem,5vw,2.75rem)] font-medium leading-[0.99] tracking-[-0.045em] text-white drop-shadow-[0_2px_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.2)]">
                    Where volcanic mists meet
                    <br />
                    <em className="font-normal text-[#AE8A66]">silent pine</em>
                </h1>
                <p className="animate-fade-in-up animation-delay-200 mt-6 max-w-[620px] font-sans text-[15px] font-light leading-7 text-white/85 sm:text-base">
                    A quiet mountain cabin in the caldera of Dieng, 2,093 m above sea level.
                </p>
            </div>

            <div id="booking" className="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-[clamp(3rem,23vh,11rem)] z-20 mx-auto w-[calc(100%-2rem)] max-w-[790px] sm:w-[78%]">
                <div className="animate-booking-enter rounded-xl border border-white/50 bg-[#F1ECE0] px-4 py-4 shadow-[0_12px_36px_rgba(0,0,0,0.24)] sm:px-6 sm:py-5">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-4 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_1fr_0.9fr_1.15fr_116px] lg:items-end lg:gap-4">
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                            <label htmlFor="booking-checkin" className="mb-1 block truncate px-1.5 font-sans text-[9px] font-semibold leading-3 tracking-[0.06em] text-[#747873]">ARRIVAL</label>
                            <div className="relative flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-sm bg-white px-3">
                                <CalendarIcon />
                                <span className="pointer-events-none truncate font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#343935]">{formatDate(bookingData.checkIn)}</span>
                                <input id="booking-checkin" name="checkIn" aria-label="Arrival date" type="date" min={today} value={bookingData.checkIn} onChange={updateBookingData} className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full cursor-pointer opacity-0" />
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                            <label htmlFor="booking-checkout" className="mb-1 block truncate px-1.5 font-sans text-[9px] font-semibold leading-3 tracking-[0.06em] text-[#747873]">DEPARTURE</label>
                            <div className="relative flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-sm bg-white px-3">
                                <CalendarIcon />
                                <span className="pointer-events-none truncate font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#343935]">{formatDate(bookingData.checkOut)}</span>
                                <input id="booking-checkout" name="checkOut" aria-label="Departure date" type="date" min={bookingData.checkIn || today} value={bookingData.checkOut} onChange={updateBookingData} className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full cursor-pointer opacity-0" />
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                            <label htmlFor="booking-guests" className="mb-1 block truncate px-1.5 font-sans text-[9px] font-semibold leading-3 tracking-[0.06em] text-[#747873]">SOJOURNERS</label>
                            <div className="flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-sm bg-white px-3">
                                <svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none" className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-[#AE8A66]" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.4" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"><circle cx="8" cy="6.5" r="2.3" /><path d="M3.75 15.25v-1.1A2.15 2.15 0 0 1 5.9 12h4.2a2.15 2.15 0 0 1 2.15 2.15v1.1zM13.1 4.5a2.3 2.3 0 0 1 0 4.3m1.25 3.25a2.1 2.1 0 0 1 1.9 2.1v1.1h-2.1" /></svg>
                                <select id="booking-guests" name="guests" value={bookingData.guests} onChange={updateBookingData} className="min-w-0 flex-1 appearance-none bg-transparent font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#343935] outline-none focus-visible:ring-1 focus-visible:ring-[#346B55]">
                                    <option value="1">1 Guest</option>
                                    <option value="2">2 Guests</option>
                                    <option value="3">3 Guests</option>
                                    <option value="4">4 Guests</option>
                                </select>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                            <label htmlFor="booking-room" className="mb-1 block truncate px-1.5 font-sans text-[9px] font-semibold leading-3 tracking-[0.06em] text-[#747873]">CHAMBER SELECTION</label>
                            <div className="flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-sm bg-white px-3">
                                <svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none" className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-[#AE8A66]" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.4" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"><path d="m2.75 8 7.25-5.25L17.25 8v8.75H2.75zM7 16.75v-5.5h6v5.5" /></svg>
                                <select id="booking-room" name="room" value={bookingData.room} onChange={updateBookingData} className="min-w-0 flex-1 appearance-none bg-transparent font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#343935] outline-none focus-visible:ring-1 focus-visible:ring-[#346B55]">
                                    {roomNames.map((name) => (
                                        <option key={name} value={name}>{name}</option>
                                    ))}
                                </select>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <Link href={bookingUrl} className="col-span-2 flex h-10 items-center justify-center rounded-sm bg-[#346B55] px-3 font-sans text-[9px] font-bold tracking-[0.09em] text-white transition-colors hover:bg-[#1E322A] lg:col-span-1">
                            BOOKING NOW
                        </Link>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>
    );
}

export default function Welcome({ rooms = [], reviews = [] }) {
    const { auth } = usePage().props;
    const [loginOpen, setLoginOpen] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
            const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            if (params.get('login') === '1' && !auth?.user) {
                setLoginOpen(true);
            }
        }
    }, [auth]);

    return (
        <>
            <Head>
                <title>The Boe Cottage Dieng - A Quiet Mountain Cabin</title>
                <meta name="description" content="Nikmati menginap di The Boe Cottage Dieng - cottage A-Frame bergaya Eropa dengan pemandangan pegunungan dan suasana yang tenang di dataran tinggi Dieng, Wonosobo." />
                <meta name="keywords" content="cottage dieng, penginapan dieng, the boe cottage, a-frame dieng, villa dieng, wisata dieng wonosobo" />
                <meta property="og:title" content="The Boe Cottage Dieng - A Quiet Mountain Cabin" />
                <meta property="og:description" content="Cottage A-Frame bergaya Eropa di jantung dataran tinggi Dieng. Pemandangan pegunungan, arsitektur premium, pengalaman menginap tak terlupakan." />
                <meta property="og:type" content="website" />
            </Head>

            <style>{`
                @media (max-width: 767px) {
                    #booking { bottom: max(2rem, 5vh); }
                }
            `}</style>

            <div style={{ minHeight: '100vh', backgroundColor: '#F1ECE0' }}>
                <Navbar onOpenLogin={() => setLoginOpen(true)} user={auth?.user} />
                <main>
                    <HeroSection rooms={rooms} />
                    <AboutSection />
                    <RoomSection rooms={rooms} />
                    <LocationSection />
                    <ReviewSection reviews={reviews} />
                </main>
                <Footer />
            </div>
            <LoginModal isOpen={loginOpen} onClose={() => setLoginOpen(false)} />
        </>
    );
}

import './bootstrap';
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';
import { createInertiaApp } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { resolvePageComponent } from 'laravel-vite-plugin/inertia-helpers';

createInertiaApp({
    resolve: (name) => resolvePageComponent(`./Pages/${name}.jsx`, import.meta.glob('./Pages/**/*.jsx')),
    setup({ el, App, props }) {
        createRoot(el).render(<App {...props} />)
    },
});

<?php

use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
use App\Http\Controllers\AvailabilityController;
use App\Http\Controllers\BookingController;
use App\Http\Controllers\RoomController;

Route::get('/user', function (Request $request) {
    return $request->user();
})->middleware('auth:sanctum');

// Public Read / Availability Endpoints (60 requests per minute)
Route::middleware('throttle:60,1')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/rooms/{room}/booked-dates', [AvailabilityController::class, 'getBookedDates']);
    Route::post('/check-availability', [AvailabilityController::class, 'checkOverlap']);
    Route::post('/midtrans/webhook', [BookingController::class, 'webhook']);
});

// Public Mutation Endpoints: Booking Creation & Guest Reviews (15 requests per minute)
Route::middleware('throttle:15,1')->group(function () {
    Route::post('/bookings', [BookingController::class, 'store']);
    Route::post('/reviews', [RoomController::class, 'storeReview']);
});

==> routes/console.php <==
<?php

use App\Models\Booking;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Inspiring;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Artisan;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schedule;

Artisan::command('inspire', function () {
    $this->comment(Inspiring::quote());
})->purpose('Display an inspiring quote');

Artisan::command('bookings:expire-unpaid', function () {
    $expiredCount = Booking::where('source', 'direct')
        ->where('status', 'pending')
        ->where('payment_status', 'unpaid')
        ->where('created_at', '<', now()->subHours(4))
        ->update([
            'status' => 'cancelled',
            'payment_status' => 'expired',
        ]);

    $this->info("Expired {$expiredCount} unpaid pending direct booking(s).");
})->purpose('Automatically cancel unpaid direct bookings older than 4 hours');

Schedule::command('sync:ical')->everyFifteenMinutes()->withoutOverlapping();
Schedule::command('bookings:expire-unpaid')->everyFiveMinutes()->withoutOverlapping();

==> routes/web.php <==
<?php

use App\Http\Controllers\AuthController;
use App\Http\Controllers\BookingController;
use App\Http\Controllers\IcalController;
use App\Http\Controllers\RoomController;
use App\Models\Booking;
use App\Models\IcalLink;
use App\Models\Review;
use App\Models\Room;
use App\Models\SeasonalRate;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
use Inertia\Inertia;

Route::get('/', function () {
    return Inertia::render('Welcome', [
        'rooms' => Room::with('images')->orderBy('id')->get(),
        'reviews' => Review::where('is_approved', true)->latest()->get(),
    ]);
})->name('home');

Route::get('/login', function () {
    return redirect('/?login=1');
})->name('login');

Route::post('/login', [AuthController::class, 'login'])->middleware('throttle:15,1');
Route::post('/logout', [AuthController::class, 'logout']);

Route::redirect('/cek-booking', '/reservation');

Route::get('/reservation', function () {
    return Inertia::render('Reservation', [
        'rooms' => Room::with('images')->orderBy('id')->get(),
        'seasonalRates' => SeasonalRate::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('start_date')->get(),
    ]);
})->name('reservation');

// Public Outbound iCal (.ics) Export Feed for Airbnb & Tiket.com (60 requests per minute)
Route::get('/ical/export/{room:slug}.ics', [IcalController::class, 'export'])
    ->middleware('throttle:60,1')
    ->name('ical.export');

Route::middleware('auth')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/admin-dashboard', function () {
        $today = now()->toDateString();
        $rooms = Room::with([
            'images',
            'bookings' => function ($query) use ($today) {
                $query->whereIn('status', ['confirmed', 'pending'])
                    ->where('check_out', '>=', $today)
                    ->orderBy('check_in');
            },
        ])->orderBy('id')->get();

        $activeBookings = Booking::whereIn('status', ['confirmed', 'pending'])->get();
        $monthlyRevenue = (float) Booking::whereIn('status', ['confirmed', 'pending'])
            ->where(function ($query) {
                $query->where('payment_status', 'paid')
                    ->orWhere('status', 'confirmed');
            })
            ->sum('total_price');

        $totalRooms = max(1, $rooms->count());
        $occupiedCount = $rooms->filter(function (Room $room) use ($today) {
            if ($room->status === 'OCCUPIED') {
                return true;
            }
            return $room->bookings->contains(function (Booking $b) use ($today) {
                $in = $b->check_in instanceof \DateTimeInterface ? $b->check_in->format('Y-m-d') : (string) $b->check_in;
                $out = $b->check_out instanceof \DateTimeInterface ? $b->check_out->format('Y-m-d') : (string) $b->check_out;
                return $in <= $today && $out > $today;
            });
        })->count();

        $occupancyPercent = round(($occupiedCount / $totalRooms) * 100, 1);

        $arrivalsToday = Booking::with('room')
            ->whereIn('status', ['confirmed', 'pending'])
            ->whereDate('check_in', $today)
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->get();

        $revenueTarget = 150000000;
        $targetPercent = min(100, (int) round(($monthlyRevenue / $revenueTarget) * 100));

        return Inertia::render('Admin/AdminDashboard', [
            'rooms' => $rooms,
            'arrivalsToday' => $arrivalsToday,
            'seasonalRates' => SeasonalRate::with('room')->where('is_active', true)->latest()->get(),
            'icalLinks' => IcalLink::with('room')->latest()->get(),
            'dashboardStats' => [
                'revenue' => [
                    'amount' => 'IDR ' . number_format($monthlyRevenue / 1000000, 1) . 'M',
                    'raw_amount' => $monthlyRevenue,
                    'target' => $targetPercent . '%',
                    'bookings' => $activeBookings->count(),
                ],
                'occupancy' => [
                    'amount' => $occupancyPercent . '%',
                    'units' => "{$occupiedCount} of {$rooms->count()} units",
                    'percent' => $occupancyPercent,
                ],
                'arrivals' => [
                    'parties' => $arrivalsToday->count(),
                    'guests' => (int) $arrivalsToday->sum(fn ($b) => (int) $b->adults + (int) $b->children),
                    'pending' => $arrivalsToday->where('check_in_status', 'pending_arrival')->count(),
                ],
            ],
        ]);
    })->name('admin.dashboard');

    Route::get('/central-bookings', function () {
        return Inertia::render('Admin/CentralBookings', [
            'bookings' => Booking::with('room')->latest()->get(),
            'rooms' => Room::with('images')->orderBy('id')->get(),
        ]);
    })->name('admin.bookings');

    // Admin AJAX / Mutation Endpoints
    Route::put('/admin/rooms/{room}', [RoomController::class, 'update']);
    Route::patch('/admin/rooms/{room}/status', [RoomController::class, 'updateStatus']);
    Route::post('/admin/seasonal-rates', [RoomController::class, 'storeSeasonalRate']);
    Route::post('/admin/bookings', [BookingController::class, 'storeManual']);
    Route::patch('/admin/bookings/{booking}', [BookingController::class, 'updateStatus']);

    // Admin OTA iCal Channel Endpoints
    Route::post('/admin/ical-links', [IcalController::class, 'store']);
    Route::delete('/admin/ical-links/{icalLink}', [IcalController::class, 'destroy']);
    Route::post('/admin/ical-links/sync', [IcalController::class, 'syncNow']);
});

==> tests/Feature/BoeCottageE2ETest.php <==
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Models\Booking;
use App\Models\IcalLink;
use App\Models\Review;
use App\Models\Room;
use App\Models\SeasonalRate;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
use Inertia\Testing\AssertableInertia as Assert;
use Tests\TestCase;

/**
 * End-to-end feature suite for The Boe Cottage booking engine,
 * Midtrans payments, admin panel and OTA iCal channel manager.
 */
class BoeCottageE2ETest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    protected bool $seed = true;

    private const SERVICE_FEE = 370000;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();

        // Ensure Midtrans runs in local fallback mode (no external HTTP calls) unless a test overrides it.
        config([
            'services.midtrans.server_key' => '',
            'services.midtrans.is_production' => false,
        ]);

        // Remove seeded seasonal rules so price assertions are deterministic.
        SeasonalRate::query()->delete();
    }

    private function daisy(): Room
    {
        return Room::where('slug', 'daisy-room')->firstOrFail();
    }

    private function lily(): Room
    {
        return Room::where('slug', 'lily-room')->firstOrFail();
    }

    private function admin(): User
    {
        return User::where('email', 'admin@theboecottage.com')->firstOrFail();
    }

    private function bookingPayload(Room $room, int $startOffset, int $endOffset, array $overrides = []): array
    {
        return array_merge([
            'room_id' => $room->id,
            'check_in' => now()->addDays($startOffset)->toDateString(),
            'check_out' => now()->addDays($endOffset)->toDateString(),
            'adults' => 2,
            'children' => 0,
            'guest_name' => 'QA Automation Guest',
            'guest_email' => 'qa.guest@example.com',
            'guest_phone' => '+62 811-0000-0000',
            'notes' => 'Automated E2E test booking.',
        ], $overrides);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. Public Pages & Inertia Props
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    public function test_home_page_renders_with_seeded_rooms_and_reviews(): void
    {
        $this->get('/')
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertInertia(fn (Assert $page) => $page
                ->component('Welcome')
                ->has('rooms', 2)
                ->where('rooms.0.name', 'Daisy Room')
                ->where('rooms.0.slug', 'daisy-room')
                ->where('rooms.1.name', 'Lily Room')
                ->where('rooms.1.slug', 'lily-room')
                ->has('rooms.0.images', 4)
                ->has('reviews', 3)
            );
    }

    public function test_reservation_page_renders_with_seeded_rooms(): void
    {
        $this->get('/reservation')
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertInertia(fn (Assert $page) => $page
                ->component('Reservation')
                ->has('rooms', 2)
                ->where('rooms.0.name', 'Daisy Room')
                ->where('rooms.1.name', 'Lily Room')
                ->has('seasonalRates')
            );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. Review Submission
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    public function test_guest_can_submit_review(): void
    {
        $this->postJson('/api/reviews', [
            'guest_name' => 'QA Reviewer',
            'rating' => 5,
            'comment' => 'Automated review: misty mornings, perfect stay.',
        ])->assertCreated()->assertJsonPath('review.guest_name', 'QA Reviewer');

        $this->assertDatabaseHas('reviews', [
            'guest_name' => 'QA Reviewer',
            'rating' => 5,
            'is_approved' => true,
        ]);
        $this->assertSame(4, Review::count());
    }

    public function test_review_validation_rejects_invalid_rating(): void
    {
        $this->postJson('/api/reviews', ['rating' => 9, 'comment' => 'x'])
            ->assertStatus(422)
            ->assertJsonValidationErrors(['rating']);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. Atomic Booking & Midtrans Fallback
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    public function test_guest_booking_is_created_with_server_side_price_and_snap_token(): void
    {
        $room = $this->daisy();

        $response = $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($room, 10, 12))
            ->assertCreated();

        $expectedTotal = (2 * 3000000) + self::SERVICE_FEE; // 6,370,000

        $code = $response->json('booking.booking_code');
        $this->assertMatchesRegularExpression('/^BOE-\d{8}-[A-Z0-9]{4}$/', $code);
        $this->assertSame('MOCK-SNAP-' . $code, $response->json('snap_token'));
        $this->assertSame(2, $response->json('pricing.nights'));
        $this->assertEquals(self::SERVICE_FEE, $response->json('pricing.service_fee'));
        $this->assertEquals($expectedTotal, $response->json('pricing.total'));

        $booking = Booking::where('booking_code', $code)->firstOrFail();
        $this->assertSame('pending', $booking->status);
        $this->assertSame('unpaid', $booking->payment_status);
        $this->assertSame('direct', $booking->source);
        $this->assertEquals($expectedTotal, (float) $booking->total_price);
        $this->assertSame('MOCK-SNAP-' . $code, $booking->snap_token);
    }

    public function test_booking_price_applies_active_seasonal_rate(): void
    {
        $room = $this->lily();
        SeasonalRate::create([
            'rule_name' => 'QA Peak +20%',
            'room_id' => $room->id,
            'start_date' => now()->addDays(30)->toDateString(),
            'end_date' => now()->addDays(31)->toDateString(),
            'rate_adjustment_percent' => 20,
            'is_active' => true,
        ]);

        // 3 nights: day 30 & 31 adjusted, day 32 base.
        $response = $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($room, 30, 33))->assertCreated();

        $expected = (3 * 3300000) + (2 * 3300000 * 0.20) + self::SERVICE_FEE;
        $this->assertEquals($expected, $response->json('pricing.total'));
    }

    public function test_booking_uses_midtrans_snap_api_when_server_key_configured(): void
    {
        config(['services.midtrans.server_key' => 'SB-Mid-server-QA']);
        Http::fake([
            'app.sandbox.midtrans.com/*' => Http::response(['token' => 'REAL-SNAP-TOKEN-123', 'redirect_url' => 'https://x'], 201),
        ]);

        $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($this->daisy(), 40, 41))
            ->assertCreated()
            ->assertJsonPath('snap_token', 'REAL-SNAP-TOKEN-123');

        Http::assertSent(function ($request) {
            return str_contains($request->url(), 'app.sandbox.midtrans.com/snap/v1/transactions')
                && $request->header('Authorization')[0] === 'Basic ' . base64_encode('SB-Mid-server-QA:')
                && $request['transaction_details']['gross_amount'] === 3000000 + self::SERVICE_FEE;
        });
    }

    public function test_booking_validation_rejects_past_dates(): void
    {
        $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($this->daisy(), -3, -1))
            ->assertStatus(422)
            ->assertJsonValidationErrors(['check_in']);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. Anti Double-Booking & Date Blocking
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    public function test_overlapping_booking_is_rejected_and_dates_are_blocked(): void
    {
        $room = $this->daisy();

        $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($room, 10, 13))->assertCreated();

        // Overlapping range (starts inside existing stay)
        $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($room, 12, 15, ['guest_email' => 'other@example.com']))
            ->assertStatus(422)
            ->assertJsonValidationErrors(['check_in']);

        // Enveloping range
        $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($room, 9, 14))
            ->assertStatus(422);

        // Back-to-back (check-in on previous check-out day) must be allowed
        $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($room, 13, 14))->assertCreated();

        // Same dates in the other room must be allowed
        $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($this->lily(), 10, 13))->assertCreated();

        $dates = $this->getJson("/api/rooms/{$room->id}/booked-dates")
            ->assertOk()
            ->json('disabled_dates');

        foreach ([10, 11, 12, 13] as $offset) {
            $this->assertContains(now()->addDays($offset)->toDateString(), $dates);
        }
        $this->assertNotContains(now()->addDays(14)->toDateString(), $dates);

        $this->postJson('/api/check-availability', [
            'room_id' => $room->id,
            'check_in' => now()->addDays(11)->toDateString(),
            'check_out' => now()->addDays(12)->toDateString(),
        ])->assertOk()->assertJsonPath('available', false);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 5. Midtrans Webhook Verification
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    public function test_signed_settlement_webhook_confirms_booking(): void
    {
        $code = $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($this->daisy(), 20, 22))
            ->assertCreated()
            ->json('booking.booking_code');

        $serverKey = 'SB-Mid-server-QA-WEBHOOK';
        config(['services.midtrans.server_key' => $serverKey]);

        $statusCode = '200';
        $grossAmount = '6370000.00';

        $payload = [
            'order_id' => $code,
            'status_code' => $statusCode,
            'gross_amount' => $grossAmount,
            'transaction_status' => 'settlement',
            'transaction_id' => 'QA-TRX-0001',
            'payment_type' => 'bank_transfer',
            'fraud_status' => 'accept',
            'signature_key' => hash('sha512', $code . $statusCode . $grossAmount . $serverKey),
        ];

        $this->postJson('/api/midtrans/webhook', $payload)
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertJsonPath('status', 'confirmed')
            ->assertJsonPath('payment_status', 'paid');

        $booking = Booking::where('booking_code', $code)->firstOrFail();
        $this->assertSame('confirmed', $booking->status);
        $this->assertSame('paid', $booking->payment_status);
        $this->assertSame('QA-TRX-0001', $booking->midtrans_transaction_id);
        $this->assertNotNull($booking->paid_at);
    }

    public function test_webhook_with_invalid_signature_is_rejected(): void
    {
        $code = $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($this->daisy(), 20, 21))
            ->json('booking.booking_code');

        config(['services.midtrans.server_key' => 'SB-Mid-server-QA-WEBHOOK']);

        $this->postJson('/api/midtrans/webhook', [
            'order_id' => $code,
            'status_code' => '200',
            'gross_amount' => '3370000.00',
            'transaction_status' => 'settlement',
            'signature_key' => str_repeat('a', 128),
        ])->assertStatus(403);

        $this->assertSame('unpaid', Booking::where('booking_code', $code)->value('payment_status'));
    }

    public function test_expire_webhook_cancels_booking_and_releases_dates(): void
    {
        $room = $this->daisy();
        $code = $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($room, 25, 27))->json('booking.booking_code');

        $serverKey = 'SB-Mid-server-QA-WEBHOOK';
        config(['services.midtrans.server_key' => $serverKey]);

        $this->postJson('/api/midtrans/webhook', [
            'order_id' => $code,
            'status_code' => '407',
            'gross_amount' => '6370000.00',
            'transaction_status' => 'expire',
            'signature_key' => hash('sha512', $code . '407' . '6370000.00' . $serverKey),
        ])->assertOk();

        $this->assertDatabaseHas('bookings', ['booking_code' => $code, 'status' => 'cancelled', 'payment_status' => 'expired']);

        // Dates are released for new guests
        config(['services.midtrans.server_key' => '']);
        $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($room, 25, 27))->assertCreated();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 6. Auth & Admin Route Protection
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    public function test_guests_are_redirected_away_from_admin_pages(): void
    {
        $this->get('/admin-dashboard')->assertRedirect('/?login=1');
        $this->get('/central-bookings')->assertRedirect('/?login=1');
        $this->postJson('/admin/ical-links/sync')->assertUnauthorized();
        $this->patchJson("/admin/rooms/{$this->daisy()->id}/status", ['status' => 'MAINTENANCE'])->assertUnauthorized();
    }

    public function test_invalid_login_is_rejected(): void
    {
        $this->postJson('/login', ['email' => 'admin@theboecottage.com', 'password' => 'wrong-password'])
            ->assertStatus(422)
            ->assertJsonValidationErrors(['email']);
        $this->assertGuest();
    }

    public function test_admin_can_login_and_access_dashboards(): void
    {
        $this->post('/login', [
            'email' => 'admin@theboecottage.com',
            'password' => 'password123',
        ])->assertRedirect('/admin-dashboard');

        $this->assertAuthenticatedAs($this->admin());

        $this->get('/admin-dashboard')
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertInertia(fn (Assert $page) => $page
                ->component('Admin/AdminDashboard')
                ->has('rooms', 2)
                ->has('dashboardStats.revenue')
                ->has('dashboardStats.occupancy')
                ->has('icalLinks')
                ->has('rooms.0.export_url')
            );

        $this->get('/central-bookings')
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertInertia(fn (Assert $page) => $page
                ->component('Admin/CentralBookings')
                ->has('bookings', 2)
                ->has('rooms', 2)
            );

        $this->post('/logout')->assertRedirect('/');
        $this->assertGuest();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 7. Admin Mutations
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    public function test_admin_can_create_seasonal_rate(): void
    {
        $this->actingAs($this->admin())
            ->postJson('/admin/seasonal-rates', [
                'rule_name' => 'QA Lebaran Peak',
                'room_id' => null,
                'start_date' => now()->addDays(50)->toDateString(),
                'end_date' => now()->addDays(55)->toDateString(),
                'rate_adjustment_percent' => 25,
            ])
            ->assertCreated()
            ->assertJsonPath('seasonal_rate.rule_name', 'QA Lebaran Peak');

        $this->assertDatabaseHas('seasonal_rates', ['rule_name' => 'QA Lebaran Peak', 'is_active' => true]);
    }

    public function test_admin_maintenance_toggle_blocks_bookings(): void
    {
        $room = $this->lily();

        $this->actingAs($this->admin())
            ->patchJson("/admin/rooms/{$room->id}/status", ['status' => 'MAINTENANCE'])
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertJsonPath('room.status', 'MAINTENANCE');

        $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($room, 60, 62))
            ->assertStatus(422)
            ->assertJsonValidationErrors(['room_id']);

        $this->postJson('/api/check-availability', [
            'room_id' => $room->id,
            'check_in' => now()->addDays(60)->toDateString(),
            'check_out' => now()->addDays(62)->toDateString(),
        ])->assertJsonPath('available', false);

        // Maintenance is reflected in the outbound OTA feed
        $ics = $this->get("/ical/export/{$room->slug}.ics")->assertOk()->getContent();
        $this->assertStringContainsString("MAINTENANCE-{$room->slug}@theboecottage.com", $ics);

        // Re-open the room
        $this->patchJson("/admin/rooms/{$room->id}/status", ['status' => 'AVAILABLE'])->assertOk();
        $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($room, 60, 62))->assertCreated();
    }

    public function test_admin_can_create_manual_reservation_and_update_status(): void
    {
        $room = $this->daisy();

        $response = $this->actingAs($this->admin())
            ->postJson('/admin/bookings', [
                'room_id' => $room->id,
                'guest_name' => 'Walk-in WhatsApp Guest',
                'guest_phone' => '+62 813-1111-2222',
                'check_in' => now()->addDays(70)->toDateString(),
                'check_out' => now()->addDays(72)->toDateString(),
                'source' => 'whatsapp',
            ])
            ->assertCreated()
            ->assertJsonPath('booking.source', 'whatsapp')
            ->assertJsonPath('booking.status', 'confirmed')
            ->assertJsonPath('booking.payment_status', 'paid');

        $bookingId = $response->json('booking.id');

        // Manual reservations also respect double-booking protection
        $this->postJson('/admin/bookings', [
            'room_id' => $room->id,
            'guest_name' => 'Conflicting Guest',
            'guest_phone' => '+62 813-0000-0000',
            'check_in' => now()->addDays(71)->toDateString(),
            'check_out' => now()->addDays(73)->toDateString(),
        ])->assertStatus(422);

        $this->patchJson("/admin/bookings/{$bookingId}", ['check_in_status' => 'checked_in'])
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertJsonPath('booking.check_in_status', 'checked_in');
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 8. OTA iCal Export & Sync
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    public function test_ical_export_returns_valid_calendar_with_active_bookings(): void
    {
        $room = $this->daisy();
        $code = $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($room, 15, 17))->json('booking.booking_code');

        $response = $this->get("/ical/export/{$room->slug}.ics?token={$room->export_token}")
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertHeader('Content-Type', 'text/calendar; charset=utf-8');

        $ics = $response->getContent();
        $this->assertStringContainsString('BEGIN:VCALENDAR', $ics);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('PRODID:-//The Boe Cottage//Channel Manager//EN', $ics);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('BEGIN:VEVENT', $ics);
        $this->assertStringContainsString("UID:{$code}@theboecottage.com", $ics);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('DTSTART;VALUE=DATE:' . now()->addDays(15)->format('Ymd'), $ics);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('DTEND;VALUE=DATE:' . now()->addDays(17)->format('Ymd'), $ics);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('END:VCALENDAR', $ics);

        // Wrong token is rejected
        $this->get("/ical/export/{$room->slug}.ics?token=invalid-token")->assertForbidden();
    }

    public function test_admin_ical_link_crud_and_sync_imports_updates_and_cancels(): void
    {
        $room = $this->lily();
        $admin = $this->admin();
        $feedUrl = 'https://www.airbnb.com/calendar/ical/qa-feed.ics';

        $start1 = now()->addDays(80)->format('Ymd');
        $end1 = now()->addDays(82)->format('Ymd');
        $start2 = now()->addDays(90)->format('Ymd');
        $end2 = now()->addDays(92)->format('Ymd');

        $buildFeed = fn (array $events) => "BEGIN:VCALENDAR\r\nVERSION:2.0\r\nPRODID:-//Airbnb Inc//Hosting Calendar//EN\r\n"
            . implode('', array_map(fn ($e) => "BEGIN:VEVENT\r\nUID:{$e[0]}\r\nDTSTART;VALUE=DATE:{$e[1]}\r\nDTEND;VALUE=DATE:{$e[2]}\r\nSUMMARY:Reserved\r\nEND:VEVENT\r\n", $events))
            . "END:VCALENDAR\r\n";

        Http::fakeSequence('www.airbnb.com/*')
            ->push($buildFeed([['airbnb-uid-1', $start1, $end1], ['airbnb-uid-2', $start2, $end2]]), 200)
            ->push($buildFeed([['airbnb-uid-1', now()->addDays(81)->format('Ymd'), now()->addDays(83)->format('Ymd')]]), 200);

        $linkId = $this->actingAs($admin)
            ->postJson('/admin/ical-links', ['room_id' => $room->id, 'platform' => 'airbnb', 'url' => $feedUrl])
            ->assertCreated()
            ->json('ical_link.id');

        // First sync: two imports
        $this->postJson('/admin/ical-links/sync')
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertJsonPath('summary.imported', 2)
            ->assertJsonPath('summary.errors', 0);

        $this->assertSame(2, Booking::where('room_id', $room->id)->where('source', 'airbnb')->where('status', 'confirmed')->count());
        $this->assertNotNull(IcalLink::find($linkId)->last_synced_at);

        // Second sync: uid-1 dates moved (update), uid-2 removed (cancel)
        $this->postJson('/admin/ical-links/sync')
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertJsonPath('summary.imported', 0)
            ->assertJsonPath('summary.updated', 1)
            ->assertJsonPath('summary.cancelled', 1);

        $moved = Booking::where('external_uid', 'airbnb-uid-1')->firstOrFail();
        $this->assertSame(now()->addDays(81)->toDateString(), $moved->check_in->format('Y-m-d'));
        $this->assertSame('cancelled', Booking::where('external_uid', 'airbnb-uid-2')->value('status'));
        $this->assertSame(1, Booking::where('external_uid', 'airbnb-uid-1')->count(), 'No duplicate on date change');

        // OTA-imported dates block direct bookings
        $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($room, 81, 82))->assertStatus(422);

        // Delete the link
        $this->deleteJson("/admin/ical-links/{$linkId}")->assertOk();
        $this->assertDatabaseMissing('ical_links', ['id' => $linkId]);
    }

    public function test_sync_ical_artisan_command_runs_cleanly(): void
    {
        $this->artisan('sync:ical')->assertSuccessful();
    }

    public function test_expire_unpaid_command_cancels_stale_direct_bookings(): void
    {
        $code = $this->postJson('/api/bookings', $this->bookingPayload($this->daisy(), 35, 36))->json('booking.booking_code');
        Booking::where('booking_code', $code)->update(['created_at' => now()->subHours(5)]);

        $this->artisan('bookings:expire-unpaid')->assertSuccessful();

        $this->assertDatabaseHas('bookings', ['booking_code' => $code, 'status' => 'cancelled']);
    }
}
